2a2(rgyud): ne lhang nger bzhugs nas/byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs chen pos kun nas bskor te/sems kyi dbang thams cad kyi dam pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa ni brnyes/sku dang gsung dang thugs ni mnyam par byin gyis 
2a3(rgyud): brlabs/g.yo ba dang rlom sems thams cad dang ni bral zhing lhun gyis grub par sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa la 'jug cing /'di lta ste/lus mnyam pa nyid kyi gnas shes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs dang /
2a4(rgyud): ngag mnyam pa nyid kyi gnas zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grang dang /de bzhin du yid mnyam pa nyid kyi gnas she bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangasakyi chos ston to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' de dag gis chos kyi rnam 
2a5(rgyud): grangs 'di thos nas/lus dang ngag dang yid mnyam pa nyid kyi bkod pa'i snying po zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin thob ste/ting nge 'dzin thob pa dang bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par 
2a6(rgyud): rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad de nyid kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul snyed kyi de bzhin gshegs pa/byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs kyis yongs su bskor 
2a7(rgyud): nas/'di lta ste/lus dang ngag dang yid kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi gnas bkod pa zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam drangs kyi chos ston pa dag mthong ngo//de ci'i phyir zhe na/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang la 
2a8(rgyud): bdag me tog gi gzhi'i snying po'i rgyan bkod pa dang ldan pa/'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byam du ma nges par ston pa 'di na byang chub tu sems bskyed pa dang spyod pa la nan tan byed pa dang /sa bsgrub pa dang byang chub 
2b1(rgyud): mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa dang /chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba dang yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa chen po ston pa de thams cad ni bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyis byin gyis brlabs kyis 
2b2(rgyud): so||mdor na sems can gang la la dag dge ba mngon par 'du bya ba byed pa de dag thams cad kyang bcom ldan 'das rnam parasnang mdzad kyis sngon byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa na/bsdu ba'i dngos po 
2b3(rgyud): bzhi po 'di lta ste/sbyin pa dang snyan par smra pa dang /don spyod pa dang don mthun pas bsdus pa sha stag ste/de dag ni rgyu de nyid dang dmigs pa de dag nyid kyis dge ba mngon par 'du byed do//de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 
2b4(rgyud): de dag bcom ldan 'das rnam paranang mdzad kyi ye shes chen po shin tu rgyas pa de thams cad mthong zhing 'jug ste/rnam par blta zhing nye bar sems te/dge ba ci tsam mngon par 'du byed pa yang rgyu de nyid dang 
2b5(rgyud): dmigs pa de nyid kyis so//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' de dag ngo mtshar du gyur cing /ya mtshan gyis 'dun pa'i snying du gyur nas/de bzhin gshegs pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab po snyam ste cang mi smra bar gyur 
2b6(rgyud): nas//bcom ldan 'das nyid la rnam par blta zhing nye bar de sems so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag ya mtshan gyis snying 'dun pa mkhyen nas/byang chub sems 
2b7(rgyud): dpa' thams cad yang dag par bskul ba zhes bya ba'i 'od zer 'khor 'od zer stong phrag du ma pa mgur nas phyung ngo //de byung nas bcom ldan 'das rnam pa snang mdzad kyi sku thams cad snang bar byas te/nam mkha'i 
2b8(rgyud): khams rab 'byam dag dang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dag na byang chub sems dpa' gang dag dbang dang ldan par gyur pa 'khod pa de dag la/thams cad mkhyen pa rdzogs sangs rgyas//bcom ldan rims nad bral ba

3b3(rgyud): gis rang rang gi byang chub sems dpa' de dag la bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu dag 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud me tog gi gzhi'i snying po'i rgyan bkod pa dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byam du ma

3b5(rgyud): rigs kyi bu dag de la mtshams sbyor ba bkod pa rnams kyi tshad ni/gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams bye ba phrag brgya dang ri'i rgyal po ri rab bye ba phrag brgya dang rgya mtsho chen po bye ba phrag brgya dang /zla ba dang nyi ma bye ba phrag

3b8(rgyud): mtshams sbyor ba bye ba phrag brgya gang gi khongs su chus pa de/me tog gi gzhi'i dang po'i rgyan bkod pa rab 'byam gyi 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud gcig go/me tog gi gzhi yi snying po'i rgyan bkod 
4a1(rgyud): ba'i rgyud bye ba phrag brgya rnams gang gi khongs su 'dus shing rjes su zhugs pa de/me tog gi gzhi'i snying po'i rgyan bkod pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud par ma gcig go/'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byams 
4a2(rgyud): par ma'i bkod pa'i rgyud bye ba phrag brgya gang gi khongs su 'dus zhing rjes su zhugs pa de//me tog gi gzhi'i snying po'i rgyun bkod pa'i 'jig rten kyi khams bkod pa cig ste/de ni bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang 
4a3(rgyud): mdzad de/sngon byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa na yongs su sbyangs so//rigs kyi bu dag 'jig rten gyi khams de na de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gcig po de 
4a4(rgyud): nyid bcom ldan 'das rnam pa snang mdzad de nyid yin te/sngon gyi smon lam gyi dbang gis byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs kyis ni yongs su bskor/lus dang ngag dang yid kyis las ni shin tu 'das shing nam mkha' 
4a5(rgyud): nyid byang chub kyi snying por byin gyis brlabs nas/'jig rten gyi khams bar ma de na rgyal po dbang sgyur gyi khang pa chen po na bzhugs so//rigs kyi bu dag bcom ldan 'das rnam par/snang mdzad de ni de 'dra ste/
4a6(rgyud): de'i yon tan nam spyod pa'am smon lam mam ye shes kyi mtha' ni nged kyis bskal pa grangs med par yang rtogs par mi nus so//rigs kyi bu dag nged kyang bcom ldan 'das de sngon byang chub sems dpar gyur pa na/

54b1(rgyud): nu lag bzang ngo //kha nas ni gzhon nu rdo rje'i sde'o//rdo rje cod pan las ni gzhon nu rdo rje cod pan no//spyi gtsug las ni gzhon nu gtsug nas byung ngo //rdo rje me tog rnams las rdo rje gzhon nu me tog go/rdo rje me tog thod las ni gzhon nu rdo rje me

55a8(rgyud): sarba thA saM b+ho d+hi badzra d+ha ra pri ya badzra gIrti swA hA/rdo rje glu'i sngags so//na maHsarba da thA ga te b+h+yaHsarba mu khe b+h+yaHsarba thA dz+wa li ti badzra mu rti badzra d+ha ra swA hA/gzhon nu rdo rje 'bar ba'i sngags so//na maHsarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaHsarba mu khi b+h+yaHsarba thA te dza a 
55b1(rgyud): ba la ma ta bi ta mu rti karta ka swA hA/gzhon nu rdo rje gnya' shing 'dzin gyi sngags so//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bka' bzhin byed pa lag na rdo rjes rdo rje las yang dag par byung ba sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad 
55b2(rgyud): kyi yum sgyu ma'i ting nge 'dzin la dbang bskur zer ba/gnod pa thams cad 'gog pa lus la bsrung ba chen po'i srad bu thogs pa rtag tu ma byed zer bar bdag gi zhes brjod pa'i sgra phyung ngo //na maHsarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaHsarba mu khe b+h+yaHsarba thA he badzra shu li ni badzra
